{D1347}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_bi ma la pra b+hA nA ma mU la tan+t+rA nu sA ri NI dwA da sha sA ha sri kA la g+hu kA la tsakra tan+t+ra rA dza TI kA/_bod skad du/_bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po dus kyi 'khor lo'i 'grel bshad/_rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rjes su 'jug pa stong phrag bcu gnyis pa dri ma med pa'i 'od ces 
[\[107b.2\]]bya ba/_dpal dus kyi 'khor lo la phyag 'tshal lo/_/stong nyid snying rje'i bdag nyid can/_/srid gsum skye dang 'jig pa med/_/shes dang shes bya gcig pa'i sku/_/dpal ldan dus 'khor la phyag 'tshal/_/rnam par bcas kyang rnam med bcom ldan 'das ma shes rab 
[\[107b.3\]]ma ste de yis 'khyud/_/skye 'jig spangs pa'i 'gyur med bde ba dang ldan bzhad pa la sogs bde ba spangs/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi skyed mdzad sku gsum dag dang ldan zhing dus gsum yang dag rig_/kun mkhyen mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan gnyis med de nyid la 
[\[107b.4\]]phyag 'tshal/_/sangs rgyas seng ge'i khrir bzhugs rdo rje'i rnal 'byor rnam par dag pa srid pa gsum gyis mchod/_/de nyid gcig pu sku yi rab tu dbye bas rnam pa bcu drug srid med srid par gnas/_/shes dang shes bya gcig gyur rgyal ba mchog gi dam tshig yan lag rnam pa 
[\[107b.5\]]bcu gnyis kyi/_/bden don byang chub sems ni rgyal ba'i rdo rje'i tshig gyur dus kyi 'khor lo la phyag 'tshal/_/kun mkhyen ye shes sku ni rgyal ba'i bdag po'i lhan cig skyes dang de bzhin chos kyi sku/_/longs spyod rdzogs dang sprul pa'i sku yang nyin byed sku dang pad+ma'i 'dab ma rgyas 
[\[107b.6\]]pa'i spyan/_/rnam par thar dang sku gsung thugs chags srid 'das 'byor pa rnal 'byor dag pa shes rab dang /_/thabs ni gnyis med gang zhig lha mi lha min gyis bstod dang po'i sangs rgyas de la 'dud/_/ye shes stong nyid ye shes kyis/_/legs dag rnam dag 'gyur ba med/_/
[\[107b.7\]]sems ni mtshan med ye shes kyis/_/legs dag gnyis med chos bdag nyid/_/dag ni smon med ye shes kyis/_/dag pa gsang sngags mi zad sgra/_/de bzhin mngon par 'dus ma byas/_/ye shes kyis dag dri ma med/_/shes rab thabs kyi bdag nyid sbyor/_/dbyibs 
[\[108a\]]
[\[108a.1\]]sku rdzu 'phrul bgrod pa can/_/de bzhin gshegs pas rdo rje sems/_/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che/_/dam tshig sems dpar gsungs pa gang /_/dngos dang dngos med zad pa'i gtso/_/thog mtha' med cing zhi ba yi/_/byang chub sems de la 
[\[108a.2\]]phyag 'tshal/_/sprul pa yi ni sku gsung thugs/_/ye shes gcig sbyor sdom pa dang /_/longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku gsung thugs/_/ye shes gcig sbyor sdom pa dang /_/dpal ldan chos kyi sku gsung thugs/_/ye shes gcig sbyor sdom pa dang /_/lhan cig skyes pa'i 
[\[108a.3\]]sku gsung thugs/_/ye shes gcig sbyor sdom pa ni/_/sad rmi gnyid stug bzhi pa yi/_/dbang po gnyis skyes bde min zhing /_/ye shes sems dang ngag dang lus/_/gnas bzhir yang dag mi gnas te/_/las kyi phyag rgya yongs dor zhing /_/ye shes phyag rgya rnam par spangs/_/
[\[108a.4\]]phyag rgya che las yang dag skyes/_/lhan skyes gzhan dang 'grogs pa min/_/rnam rtog bsgom pa las 'das pa/_/phyag rgya chen po mi 'gyur bde/_/gzung dang 'dzin pa dbyibs dag dang /_/rtog dang brjod pa rnam par spangs/_/dri za'i grong khyer rnam pa dang /_/pra phab pa yi rang 
[\[108a.5\]]bzhin can/_/thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid sbyor/_/e waM yi ge de la 'dud/_/phra rab rdul gyi chos nyid 'das/_/pra phab pa yi rang bzhin can/_/rnam pa kun gyi mchog ldan ma/_/phyag rgya chen mo de la 'dud/_/sangs rgyas kun gyi skyed mdzad ma/_/skye ba dang ni 'jig pa 
[\[108a.6\]]spangs/_/kun du bzang po'i spyod pa can/_/sna tshogs yum de la phyag 'tshal/_/A li kA li snyoms 'jug dang /_/hUM phaT yi ge sogs spangs kyang /_/'gyur ba med las 'khrungs pa'i sku/_/dus kyi 'khor lo de la 'dud/_/kun mkhyen ye shes sku zhes pa/_/khyab bdag nyi ma lta bu'i 
[\[108a.7\]]sku/_/pad+ma'i 'dab ma rgyas pa'i spyan/_/de nyid bcu drug dbye ba las/_/sku bzhi'i bdag nyid sangs rgyas ni/_/rdo rje seng ge'i gdan bzhugs pa/_/lha dang lha min gyis bstod sngon/_/zla bzang gis gsol de la 'dud/_/ngag dang ye shes gcig sbyor rgyal ba mchog gi dam tshig 
[\[108b\]]
[\[108b.1\]]thugs dang chos ni gcig tu sbyor/_/gsung dang longs spyod rdzogs pa gcig sbyor srid gsum gyis mchod sku dang sprul pa rnal 'byor te/_/shes dang shes bya gnyis med srid 'das 'byor pa 'di ni rdo rje sems dpa' rnam pa bzhi/_/rdo rje'i rnal 'byor thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid lha mi lha 
[\[108b.2\]]min gyis bstod de la 'dud/_/zhabs gcig rdo rje nor bur rdul dang nyi ma dang /_/gnyis pa gtsug tor khu ba ri bong can la ni/_/rtag tu mi chod dbyer med 'dod par bkod gyur pa/_/'jig rten gsum gyis mchod pa'i gtso la spyi bos 'dud/_/rlung me chu dang sa yi dkyil 'khor lhun po pad+ma 
[\[108b.3\]]dang /_/zla nyi dus me'i steng du dbang phyug dang ni bdud kyi mgor/_/zhabs ni srid pa'i yum gyi zhabs dang lhan cig bkod gyur pa/_/lha yi dbang pos btud pa'i gtso la spyi bos phyag 'tshal te/_/de ltar rdo rje'i rnal 'byor gang zhig dpag med bde gshegs rnams kyis bsten cing bstan pa 
[\[108b.4\]]dang /_/byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje mchog 'dzin gsang sngags rig pa kun gyis yang dag brjod gyur pa'i/_/'gyur med mchog gi bde ba rnal 'byor de ni dus kyi 'khor lor blun po bdag brjod 'dod/_/mkha' la myur 'gro mkha' 'gro'i bdag po 'gro ste 'dab chags gzhan pa 'gro ba 
[\[108b.5\]]min nam ci/_/gang du rang gi lus la srid pa mtha' dag sngags bcas pa/_/gsal bar A li thig le 'dzin pa rgyal ba'i dbang pos gsungs/_/chu gter chen po der ni rkyal ba'i slad du rab zhugs pa/_/de la gal te pha rol bdag phyin rgyal ba'i mthu yin no/_/gang phyir gsang bdag 
[\[108b.6\]]la gsungs mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas las/_/stobs bcu pa yis bsdus pa'i rgyud ni sngon du lung bstan pa/_/de phyir dus kyi 'khor lo bsdus pa'i rgyud 'di ka lA par/_/'jam dpal gyis ni thub pa mtha' dag rnams la nges par bstan/_/'grel bshad stong phrag drug cu pa/_/zla ba 
[\[108b.7\]]bzang pos bris pa gang /_/theg pa kun don gsal byed de/_/pad+ma dkar po bdag gis ni/_/rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rjes 'jug cing /_/gzhung ni stong phrag bcu gnyis te/_/rdo rje'i tshig 'byed dang bcas pa/_/bsdus pa'i rgyud la bri bar bya/_/rgyal bas gsungs pa'i cho gas dbang mchog blangs nas 
[\[109a\]]
[\[109a.1\]]rgyud 'di'am ni bsdus pa yi/_/rgyud rgyal ma lus thos nas yang dag ye shes lam la gnas pa'i rnal 'byor pas/_/khengs dang longs spyod 'byor pa du ma btang nas sems ni bla mar gtad byas te/_/sangs rgyas nyid kyi slad du gtso bo'i 'grel bshad gzhan phan spyod pa 
[\[109a.2\]]mnyan par bya/_/mngon shes med gang dpal ldan rdo rje theg pa la gnas bstan bcos du ma yi/_/rnam rtog chos kyi snying dang mkhas pa nyid kyi dregs ldan 'grel bshad chen po dag_/byed pa de ni glang chen long ba myos bzhin dgra yi rgyud ni rgyal ba'i slad/_/gang gnas de yis rang gi rgyud ni 
[\[109a.3\]]nyams byed 'di ni rtag tu bdud kyi gnyen/_/mkhas pa'i mngon pa'i nga rgyal dang ldan rgyal bdag gsung ni mi shes ngang tshul rtag long bas/_/theg mchog 'grel bshad byas te long ba gzhan dag lhung du bcug nas dmyal bar rab 'jug pa/_/'gyod pa dang ni srog gcod la sogs mi dge'i lam 
[\[109a.4\]]la bud med bde bas sems drangs shing /_/dpal ldan shes rab bde nyams rdo rje'i tshig bcom phyi yi longs spyod dag gis zil mnan pa'o/_/gang zhig ston pa nyid kyis lung bstan rgyal bdag bde ster rdo rje theg par mtha' dag ni/_/'grel bshad dag dang bsdu ba mdzad pa rdo rje mchog 
[\[109a.5\]]'dzin bdag 'dra'i byang chub sems dpa' ste/_/de dag 'dir ni lha dang lag 'gro mi rnams smin pa'i don du sangs rgyas mthur smra la/_/gang dag mngon shes lnga med de dag bdag cag rgyal dbang yin zhes bden pa min pa smra/_/ma 'ongs dus na rtag tu rtog ge la sogs bstan bcos la 
[\[109a.6\]]gtad sna tshogs blos/_/sangs rgyas pa yang gzhan gyi rtog ge dang phrad dbang gis log pa 'byung 'gyur te/_/rtog ge la gtad de yis 'dod pa'i yul la lam ni ston par byed pa gang /_/de dag nyid kyi blo ngan skyon ni 'joms byed 'grel bshad bdag gis bri bar bya/_/chags bral med 
[\[109a.7\]]pa'i 'dod chags mtha' yas chu la zhe sdang sbrul dang chu srin dkrigs/_/gti mug klong ni rab mang 'gro 'ong nor dang dpal la brkam pa'i rba rlabs can/_/'gyod sogs gsod pa la sogs las kyi rta gdong 'khor mo sgra drag srid pa yi/_/chu gter la ni dpal ldan rdo rje theg pa'i 'grel bshad 
[\[109b\]]
[\[109b.1\]]rtag tu mnyan pa ste/_/shes rab rlung gi dar gyis myur 'gro myang 'das pha rol don gnyer chen po rnams/_/dam pa'i bde bas myur du rgal bar byed slad de ni lam gyi 'dren ma dang /_/nyon mongs bdud tshogs 'joms pa mkhas pa'i yang dag ye shes yid bzhin nor bu ste/_/'jig 
[\[109b.2\]]rten gsum gyi bdag po nyid ster 'di ni kun du ston par byed pa'o/_/gang zhig phung po la sogs sgrib pa rab bcom yul ni pra phab brgyad lta bu/_/kun mkhyen bde ster de nyid gcig pu rdo rje 'dzin pa'i phyag rgya chen mo ste/_/de bor sangs rgyas nyid slad nor la chags shing 'khyog po'i snying 
[\[109b.3\]]ldan brtags ma dag_/gang gis sten byed rnam brtags blo can de yis de yang 'pho ba thob par byed/_/'dod chags nyams las sdig ste gang phyir shin tu sdug ma las ni zhe sdang skye/_/sdang las gti mug 'di las rang gi rdo rje lhung las sems ni rtag rmongs te/_/mchog min ma lus 
[\[109b.4\]]bza' dang btung ba la sogs yul gzhan dag la rab tu 'jug_/de yis sems la co dris bde ba nyams shing skye ba drug tu 'khor bar 'gyur/_/kun mkhyen lam don gnyer ba rnams la sgra dang sgra min rnam dpyad chen po med/_/sna tshogs yul skad ngan pa yis kyang chen po rnams kyi lam la 
[\[109b.5\]]rtag tu 'jug_/sems can rnams kyi mos pa'i sems kyi dbang gis thams cad mkhyen pa'i gsung gzhan la/_/lung ston dag la lha klus bkod pa'i sgra sogs rtsod pa don gnyer rnams kyi gzhan/_/yul gyi skad dang zur chag sgra las kyang /_/rnal 'byor ldan pas don ni 'dzin byed de/_/
[\[109b.6\]]chu la 'o ma nges par zhugs pa de/_/ngang pas rab tu phyung nas 'thung bar byed/_/don dam de nyid yul la rtag tu ni/_/chen po rnam dag tshig la rton pa min/_/yul gyi ming rnams kyis ni don shes la/_/bstan bcos sgra dag gis ni ci zhig bya/_/gang zhig zur chag sgra dang sgrar gyur pas/_/
[\[109b.7\]]brjod pa de nyid ye shes ma yin zhing /_/gang zhig 'gro ba dag la nyi tshe ba/_/de ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i gsung ma yin/_/mchog tu mi 'gyur bzhi pa de nyid ni/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi shes rab ye shes te/_/gang zhig de ni slar yang de bzhin 'di/_/rang gi phyag rgya chen mor rgyal bas 
[\[110a\]]
[\[110a.1\]]gsungs/_/shes rab 'grogs las gang gi dkar po zag_/de yi bde ba ci yis 'phel bar 'gyur/_/dpyid dang 'grogs la me tog ma rgyas pa/_/ltung ba tsu ta'i 'bras bu ci yis 'byung /_/gang gis 'gyur med ma thob sdug bsngal can/_/de ni 'gyur ba dang 
[\[110a.2\]]bcas bde ba tshol/_/thams cad chu ni tshol byed tsA ta ka_/skom par gyur kyang sa la gnas pa min/_/'khor ba yi ni bde ba mi rtag pa/_/'thob pa min yang rmongs chen tshol bar byed/_/yang dag rgyal srid bde ba thob pa yang /_/thar pa'i don du mkhas pas 'dor bar byed/_/ji ltar 
[\[110a.3\]]lo yi mtshams su rgya lam na/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]zas can 'khrig pa'i bde ba tshol/_/rdo yi phye ma dag gi kha zas can/_/phug ron rnams ni rtag tu byed pa ste/_/lan cig rtag tu khu ba ltung ba'i phyir/_/gnyis ka la yang mchog gi bde ba med/_/de bzhin dka' thub can dang 'dod ldan la/_/
[\[110a.4\]]rmi lam sad pa dag las 'dzag phyir ro/_/ji ltar gnyid log la yang sbrul gyi ni/_/sos btab mi 'tsho de bzhin dka' thub pa/_/khu ba sdud pa med phyir stobs med ma'i/_/skye gnas de yis nyams par byas pa'o/_/ji ltar rdo dreg skud pa'i gos dag ni/_/me yis bsregs pa nyid 
[\[110a.5\]]kyis dag 'gyur ba/_/de bzhin skyes bu rnams kyi dam pa yi/_/sems ni shes rab ye shes dag gis so/_/ji ltar dngul chu me yis reg pa las/_/'bros par byed kyi g.yo ba med mi 'gyur/_/de bzhin thabs dang bral ba'i khu ba ni/_/rtag tu skye gnas reg pa dag las so/_/ji ltar 
[\[110a.6\]]g.yo ba de yang sman sogs kyi/_/stobs kyis me dang phrad pas yang dag 'ching /_/de bzhin khu ba shin tu rnal 'byor gyis/_/shes rab dag dang yang dag phrad pas so/_/ji ltar me ni shing la rtag gnas kyang /_/gcad dang dbye ba'i thabs kyis mi mthong ste/_/gtsub shing gtsub stan lag pas 
[\[110a.7\]]gtsubs pa las/_/de la gnas pa dag kyang mthong 'gyur ba/_/de bzhin sems kyi 'od gsal rtog pa yi/_/sgom pa'i thabs rnams dag gis mi mthong ste/_/de nyid du 'di rkyang ma dag dang ni/_/ro ma gcig tu sbyor bas mthong bar 'gyur/_/sna tshogs rnam rtog la sogs lam gyis ni/_/
[\[110b\]]
[\[110b.1\]]de nyid de bzhin gshegs pas gang gsungs pa/_/bcu drug bzhi yi rab tu dbye ba can/_/lam dang bral bas 'thob pa ma yin no/_/bskyed pa'i rim par gsungs pa'i hU~M dang ni/_/phaT yig la sogs rtog pa dang bral ba/_/rdzogs pa'i rim pa'i rnal 'byor de nyid de/_/de nyid kyi 
[\[110b.2\]]ni sgrub thabs gzhan yod min/_/du ba la sogs mtshan ma dag dang ni/_/srog rtsol dag gis dbu mar 'bab pa dang /_/rig ma'i brtul zhugs dag gis rdo rje ni/_/ltung min de bzhin steng du khu ba ste/_/lam 'di dag gis sangs rgyas rnams kyi ni/_/bde ba mchog tu 'gyur ba med pa 
[\[110b.3\]]zhes/_/mngon par brjod pa'am pho nya mo kun gyi/_/lhan cig skyes pa rnal 'byor pas thob 'gyur/_/gzugs la sogs par rtog pa dkyil 'khor gyi/_/'khor lo bsgom pa goms par gyur pas ni/_/grub pa 'jig rten pa dag 'grub 'gyur te/_/'dod pa'i dngos grub chen po 
[\[110b.4\]]ga la zhig_/gang zhig sems can rnams la brtse ba yis/_/gzhan don dge la rtag tu brtson pa yi/_/slob ma dag pa rnams la lam dag ni/_/bla ma dam pa'i drin lan 'byung ba yin/_/lam bzang rin chen 'di ni bag med kyi/_/sems can gzhan pa dag la byin pa na/_/rang khyim 
[\[110b.5\]]dgon pa dag tu rab zhugs la/_/yul ngan chom rkun rnams kyis 'phrog par byed/_/the tshom 'gyod pa dag dang gnyid dang ni/_/le lo rgod pa'i chom rkun rnams kyis kyang /_/gnyen 'dun thibs po'i nang du zhugs pa yi/_/lam bzang rin chen dag ni 'phrog par byed/_/srog gcod 
[\[110b.6\]]ma byin gzhan gyi bud med dang /_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]dang tshig rtsub dag dang phra ma dang /_/'khyal par gyur pa'i tshig dang brnab sems dang /_/gnod sems lta ba ngan pa'i chom rkun dang /_/yod pa'i sdig dang nye ba'i sdig pa dang /_/gsod 'khu dbang po mngon par zhen pa ste/_/chom rkun lnga pa lnga 
[\[110b.7\]]po 'di dag gis/_/rin po che ni rtag tu 'phrog par byed/_/shin tu bza' dang btung ba sna tshogs dang /_/longs spyod du ma'i chom rkun de dag gis/_/brtson 'grus ldan du zin kyang bag med cing /_/brgyal bar gyur pa nyid la 'phrog par byed/_/rang nyid kyis ni yongs su ma brtags par//
[\[111a\]]
[\[111a.1\]]/rin thang chen por gyur pa yin ma yin/_/shes par bya phyir g.yon can gzhan la ston/_/de yi tshig gis rin chen 'dor bar byed/_/gang dag rin chen bzang po rig pa rnams/_/rin chen chen po yongs su rtog tu gzhug_/de dag rnams kyi rnam dag tshig gis ni/_/rang 
[\[111a.2\]]gi rin chen rnam par shes par bya/_/rdzogs sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems rnams dang /_/pha dang ma dang bu mo sring mo dang /_/bu dang spun zla 'dod pa'i chung ma yi/_/gzugs rnams dag gis bdud ni bgegs byed do/_/de phyir bla ma dam pas gnang ba yi/_/lam bzang rin chen 
[\[111a.3\]]dag ni slob ma yis/_/chom rkun rtsa lag yongs su btang byas te/_/'bad pas shin tu legs par sba bar bya/_/dpa' bo'i rim pa bdag byin brlab pa yi/_/rim pa dag kyang thar pa'i lam ma yin/_/shin tu rnam dag rim pa gcig pu ni/_/thar pa'i slad du sangs rgyas rnams kyis 
[\[111a.4\]]bstan/_/mtshams med lnga po dag ni byas pa rnams/_/dbang don bde ba nyid ni gang zhig gis/_/thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid rgyud dag la/_/rgyal ba'i dbang pos yang dag gzung bar gyur/_/dpa' bo'i rim pa phyi rol la min te/_/lus la srog zad nyid la 'di gsungs so/_/
[\[111a.5\]]bdag byin brlab pa dag ni stong pa la/_/khams gsum po ni mthong bar gyur pa'i mtshan/_/'khor ba snying po med de zad gyur pa/_/de las skyes bu'i sangs rgyas nyid 'bras te/_/chu shing gi ni 'bras bu smin pa dang /_/chu shing nyams pas yang dag 'byung ba bzhin/_/sdig pa dang ni shing srin 
[\[111a.6\]]sems can rnams/_/gang las byung ba de dag de nyams byed/_/de bzhin rnal 'byor dag gi sems de ni/_/sgyu ma rnam par nyams par byed pa yin/_/gnyis med g.yo ba med dang rigs med dang /_/'dod dang mchog tu mi 'gyur 'dod chags che/_/dngos dang dngos po med pa med pa dang /_/
[\[111a.7\]]ye shes dang ni sems dpa' kun du bzang /_/nyon mongs med dang nyon mongs chen po dang /_/lhan skyes dpal ldan byang sems thig le 'dzin/_/dpal ldan dus kyi 'khor lo rdo rje dang /_/thabs dang shes rab bdag nyid rnal 'byor ro/_/snying rje dang ni stong nyid sku/_/dus ni kun rdzob 
[\[111b\]]
[\[111b.1\]]gzugs can ma/_/stong nyid 'khor lo zhes brjod de/_/dus kyi 'khor lo gnyis med 'dod/_/'di yi dpal ni myur thob slad/_/rtag tu rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs/_/rnal 'byor gang zhig sgom pa de'i/_/bgegs ni rtag tu nyams par 'gyur/_/dgra dang seng ge glang chen dbang po me 
[\[111b.2\]]dang ltos 'gro'i bdag dang chom rkun zhags pas 'ching /_/chu gter 'khrug dang sha za 'chi ba'i 'jig byed nad dang dbang po'i nye bar 'tshe ba dang /_/dbul dang bud med dang bral mi bdag 'khrugs pa'i 'jigs dang rdo rje ltung dang nor nyams rnams/_/gang zhig rnal 'byor ma rnams 
[\[111b.3\]]zhabs ni gsal bar dran byed de la rab tu nyams par 'gyur/_/glang chen shing rta rta dang rab tu mang ba'i mi mchog thams cad lus la go bgos shing /_/lag na mdung dang 'khor lo ral gri mda' gzhus dgra rnams kyis ni g.yul du 'joms byed pa/_/'chi ster drag po'i gzugs 
[\[111b.4\]]can phyogs dang mtshams son dgra de thams cad rang gi mtshon gyis ni/_/bcom ste rgyal ba'i grags pa gang zhig rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dran byed pas kun nas 'thob/_/kun nas seng ge'i sgra sgrogs shin tu rnam par 'gyur ba'i bzhin ldan mche ba rnon po gtsigs/_/rnga ma g.yo zhing 
[\[111b.5\]]sder mo gug la brtan pas glang chen myos pa'i klad pa rab tu 'gems/_/rab khros nyi ma 'char ka'i mig can ro 'dzin ral gri g.yo ba'i gsod 'dod ral pa can/_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de la ring du 'gro bar 'gyur/_/mdog sngo mig ni ser skya 
[\[111b.6\]]'gram pa gnyis po myos pa'i chu bo rab tu mang bas gang /_/rnam mang ljon pa mchog rnams rtsad nas 'byin zhing rab tu grugs byed 'brug bzhin sgra dag sgrogs/_/lag pas bcing ba byas nas so yi rtse mo dag gis 'byed byed rab mchog glang po'i bdag_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams 
[\[111b.7\]]zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de yi lus ni gtong bar byed/_/kun nas me lce rab 'bar phyogs dang mtshams su du ba kun gyis mun pa chen po gtibs/_/grong dang dgon pa dus gcig sreg cing skar mda' ltung ba bzhin du me stag 'phro bar byed/_/sems can rnams la dus kun 
[\[112a\]]
[\[112a.1\]]du ni 'chi ba'i 'jigs byed phyogs kun khyab par gyur pa'i me/_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de la bsil ba nyid du 'gyur/_/khros shing mig sman sngon po'i mdog dang 'khyog por bgrod pa'i lus can drag po'i phaT sgra sgrogs/_/
[\[112a.2\]]mche ba 'khyog po lce gnyis dang ldan 'chi ba'i 'jigs byed rlung gi shugs can rab tu gtum/_/drag po'i mig dang gdengs ka gsal byas yang dag phrad pa za bar byed pa'i gdengs can dbang /_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de la rab tu nyams par 
[\[112a.3\]]'gyur/_/sdang ba'i sems ldan lag pa'i mthil na gzhu dang rna ba'i bar du mda' bkang mdung dang ni/_/'khor lo ral gri lag thogs phyogs dang mtshams su gnas pas dam pa'i lam gyi sa bkag nas/_/dgon par lam 'gron skye bo rgyu ba dang phrad 'joms byed drag po'i mig can 
[\[112a.4\]]chom rkun pa/_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de la ring ba nyid du 'gyur/_/lag pa dag ni zhags pas bcings shing rkang pa'i pad+ma zung ni lcags sgrog dag gis gtams/_/dge ba'i chu dang bral bas lce dang mgrin pa mchu skams lus po skem zhing 
[\[112a.5\]]bkres pas gzir/_/nyin mtshan dus su rje bo khros pa'i bka' yis kun du srung ma rnams kyis srung byed pa/_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de yang de las grol bar 'gyur/_/chu gter bzhon pa la zhon mtha' yas drag po'i rlung gis chu yi gter ni 
[\[112a.6\]]'khrug byed cing /_/kun du 'khor bar byed la chu gter chom rkun chu srin nya yi rigs kyis bkag gyur te/_/me yi til mar gdug pas 'phangs pa me yi char chen kun nas rgyun du 'bab pa la/_/gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de yang de las pha rol 'gro/_/
[\[112a.7\]]phaT ces 'jigs rung sgra sgrogs kha nas me 'bar lag na gri gug dang ni thod pa thogs/_/ro langs gdug pa'i sems ldan phyogs bcu'i dkyil 'khor dag la yang dag gnas shing 'jigs par byed/_/bkres pas gzir te sha dag za zhing mi khrag la dga' lus skams gos dang bral ba dag_/
[\[112b\]]
[\[112b.1\]]gang gis rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs dag 'jigs 'phrog dran pa de la srung ba byed par 'gyur/_/nyin bzhin 'khun zhing sor mo'i rtse mo zlum po nyams te sna dang mchu dang rna ba nyams/_/gdong dang mgrin pa rkang lag dag gi rma rnams las ni rnag rul mang po rab tu 'bab/_/rul pa'i 
[\[112b.2\]]dri nga'i mthu las gnyen bshes tshogs kyis shi ba'i lus bzhin yongs su btang gyur pa/_/gang zhig rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs bzang dran byed nad kyis btang zhing 'dod dang ldan par 'gyur/_/'bar bcas mche rnon gdengs can 'phrog byed sha ra b+ha dang stag dang glang chen gdong ldan 
[\[112b.3\]]zhing /_/lag na 'khor lo ral gri mda' dang rtse gsum lcags kyu rdo rje thogs pas phyogs kun du/_/byang chub bsam gtan gcig gzhol mchog bde la gnas sgrub pa po sdigs dbang po'i pho nya gang /_/de dag rnal 'byor dbang phyug ma zhabs gang gi rang yid la byed de la nyams 
[\[112b.4\]]par 'gyur/_/dbyar ni nags nas grong gi bar du 'dam rdzab kyis gang lam dag la yang khur khyer zhing /_/dgun ni gos dang bral bas kha ba'i lam du bgrod cing yul gzhan dag tu'ang 'gro bar byed/_/so ga chu med 'jigs bcas mya ngan lam du nyi ma'i 'od zer gyis gdungs dbul po gang /_/rnal 
[\[112b.5\]]'byor ma rnams zhabs bzang sgom byed sdug bsngal de dag gis btang 'dod dang ldan par 'gyur/_/mchan khung mig dang rna ba sna dang kha lus brjod pa dag las mi bzad dri 'byung zhing /_/bud med kun dang bral bas dus kun du ni dga' ba'i bde ba dang bral skal ngan te/_/mi dge 'bras bu'i 
[\[112b.6\]]dbang gis rtag tu bud med sdang bar gyur pa gang la de dag kun/_/rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs ni bde ba mchog sbyin bsgoms pas skal bzang kha dog gzugs can 'gyur/_/'khus phyir g.yog 'khor gang la mi dbang 'khrugs te 'jigs pa'i gshin rje lta bur mtha' gcig tu/_/gsod pa 
[\[112b.7\]]la zhen 'chi ba'i 'jigs pa byed pa'i sde rnams myur du yang dag skul byed pa/_/rnal 'byor ma rnams zhabs ni 'jig rten gsum po kun gyi mgon dag rang yid la bsgoms pas/_/de dag rnams kyi de yang rab tu zhi zhing 'dod pa thams cad sbyin pas mchog sbyin 'gyur/_/gang dag 'ga' zhig 
[\[113a\]]
[\[113a.1\]]tu ni mkha' las rdo rje rab ltung bud med 'grogs las rtag par yang /_/skye dang skye ba gzhan slad 'chi dang brgyal dang chags bral slar yang byed pas rnal 'byor ma'i/_/dpal ldan las dang ye shes mchog gi chu skyes la gnas rdo rje'i zhabs dag bsgoms pa 
[\[113a.2\]]yis/_/de dag rnams kyi de yang rab zhi 'chi ba nyid 'gyur slar yang skye ba ldan pa min/_/gang dag nyin dang mtshan mor bud med 'grogs la rnam par chags pas nor kun nyams 'gyur zhing /_/skye ba gzhan gyis bag chags bsgos pa'i sems las de ni slar yang mang po rab 'byung 
[\[113a.3\]]bas/_/shes rab rnal 'byor ma rnams dag gi mi mnyam mnyam pa'i zhal ni sna tshogs gzugs can gang /_/rgyal ba mchog gis mchod cing bstod pa bsgoms pas de ni nor kun nyams par mi 'gyur ro/_/ser skya lug dang kun 'dar ma ste nyi zla sgra gcan lam gsum bgrod 
[\[113a.4\]]rnams rnal 'byor ma/_/dngos dang dngos med rab nyams mnyam bgrod zhabs de dag ni de dag rnams kyi ye shes sku/_/de yi lhan skyes bde ba'i go 'phang 'gyur med gcig 'di de dag de nyid gang gis dran/_/de yi skye ba 'di la ma lus 'jigs 'phrog 'dir brjod 'bras bu myur du 
[\[113a.5\]]'byung bar 'gyur/_/dpal ldan rnal 'byor ma rnams dag gi gnas ni rdo rje nor bu'i khyim ste der 'jug lam/_/nyon mongs med cing bdud nyams sngon du rgyal bas gsungs pa'i bstod pa bdag gis 'dir brjod do/_/skyes bu gang dag bdud kyi grogs kyis rtag tu mi bden mun par song 
[\[113a.6\]]zhing lam nyams pa/_/de dag rnams kyis lam 'di thob nas rdo rje nor bu'i khyim dang ring min 'ong gyur cig_/du ba la sogs lam gyis lhan skyes bde ba'i go 'phang bsgoms na de ni dus 'khor lo/_/sum cu rtsa gnyis mtshan gyi yan lag 'gro ba'i bla ma chos kyi 'khor lo 'di la gnas/_/
[\[113a.7\]]lha rnams mgo yi gtsug gi nor bu'i 'od zer tshogs kyis zhabs kyi pad+ma la gtugs shing /_/dam pa'i lam ni ston byed nyin mor byed pa bzhin du mun pa thams cad 'joms par 'gyur/_/lha klu mi dang lha min dbang po'i cod pan dag gis zhabs pad la gtugs gang /_/yang dag

[\[113b.1\]]ye shes nyin byed 'gro ba gsum gyi dpal ldan chos kyi 'khor lor gnas/_/bdud dang nyon mongs [{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]rab gtum gsod byed dpal ldan shAkya seng ge de'i/_/rtag tu 'gro la brtse dang de yi shes rab kyis bskul 'grel bshad bdag gis bri/_/kA yig las ni rgyu zhi ba/_/

[\[113b.3\]]lo/_/rdo rje'i rnal 'byor thams cad nas/_/bden pa gnyis la gnas pa 'di/_/mngon par brjod pa'i rjod byed 'gyur/_/bde bar gshegs pas lung bstan zhing /_/'jam dpal gyis ni bskul gyur pa/_/'jig rten mgon po bdag gis ni/_/rgyud 'di'i 'grel bshad bri bar bya/_/

[\[114a.5\]]pa'i bcu dang /_'jig rten las 'das pa'i bcu gcig pa rab tu gsal bar byed pa/_las kyi phyag rgya dang ye shes kyi phyag rgya dang phyag rgya chen mo dang 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i dngos grub rab tu gsal bar byed pa/_'jig rten gyi khams dang nang dang mngon par dbang bskur ba

[\[114a.7\]]ma 'ongs pa'i dus su tshangs pa'i drang srong rnams gdul bar bya ba'i don du 'grel bshad byed pa la bdag dang /_rgyud ston pa'i don du gshin rje mthar byed la sogs pa gzhan rnams dang /_sham+b+ha la'i yul la sogs pa grong khyer bye ba phrag dgu bcu rtsa drug na gnas pa'i sems can thams cad dam

[\[114b.1\]]pa'i lam thob pa la de bzhin gshegs pas lung bstan to/_/'dir rdul phra rab 'dus pa sngon du 'gro ba ni 'jig rten gyi khams so/_/'jig rten gyi khams sngon du 'gro ba ni sems can rnams so/_/sems can sngon du 'gro ba ni 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i mngon par dbang

[\[114b.7\]]dang rjod par byed pa dang dgos pa'i dgos pa la sogs pa zhus pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_stong pa chen po'i yi ge lnga la sogs pa dang sna tshogs 'byung ba dang dkyil 'khor dang dbang bskur ba gsol ba 'debs pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_ston pa po'i lan gyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas 
[\[115a\]]
[\[115a.1\]]pa dang /_'jig rten gyi khams dang gza' dang rgyu skar dang sems can dang sngags dang srog la sogs pa 'byung ba dang sdud pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_rlung la sogs pa'i dkyil 'khor dang rgya mtsho dang gling dang ri'i grangs kyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_srid pa

[\[115b.1\]]rdzogs pa'i byang chub pa bzhis mngal 'byung ba'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_dbugs drug brgya lhag pa'i stong phrag nyi shu rtsa gcig dang /_rtsa stong phrag bdun cu rtsa gnyis dang tshigs kyi gnas sum brgya drug cu dang khams drug dang ro drug dang dbang po drug dang yul drug dang las kyi dbang po

[\[116a.1\]]yongs su shes pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_'jig rten pa dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i dbang sbyin pa'i ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_thams cad rab tu gnas pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi ngo bo nyid kyis gnas pa dang /_dam tshig gi 'khor lo 'dus

[\[149a.4\]]gcig tu 'dres par gyur pa nyid ni ye shes te stong pa chen po yi ge gsum pa'o/_/'di'i ming gi mtshan ma ni dbus ma gzhom du med pa'i mtshan ma'i lho ru thig le gnyis te brjod du med pa'o/_/thig le zhes pa'i ming 'dis stong pa bzhi pa rtogs par bya ste snga ma'i kyang

[\[183a.1\]]las grub pa'i mtha' rnams rnam par nyams zhes pa'i tshig gis bsdus te/_rgyud las gsal por ma mdzad do/_/de ci'i phyir zhe na/_'dir mu stegs pa rnams kyis bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po mthong ste/_nyi ma'i longs spyod rnam par dag pa mthong nas/_bdag

[\[183b.1\]]zhes pa ni nya'i longs spyod kyi don du phyogs gcig bsres pa dang gnam stong gi longs spyod kyi don du phyogs gnyis bsres pa phyogs kyi phung por 'gyur ro/_/mkha' dus chu yis rab phye zhes pa ni/_sgra gcan gyi phyogs kyi longs spyod kyi don du phyogs kyi 'khor lo phyogs bzhi brgya drug cus bgos

[\[244a.1\]]bar 'gyur ro/_/ri bong 'dzin pa la sgyu 'phrul dra ba dang /_'dus pa rnam pa dgu po mtha' dag phyogs kyi dbye ba ste lha'i tshogs bzhi bcu lhag pa'i lnga brgya rnams kyis so/_/de ltar nyi ma dang zla ba'i lha'i tshogs gcig tu gyur pa rnams kyis kyang dang po'i sangs

[\[244a.3\]]cha'o/_/mnyam pa'i cha ste nyin phyed nas mtshan phyed dang mtshan phyed nas nyin phyed la rgyu skar phyed dang bcas pa'i bcu gsum gyis te/_dbyug gu'am chu tshod bcu lhag pa'i brgyad brgya rnams kyis mtshan mo'i cha'o/_/de bzhin du nyin mo'i cha ste dpal ldan rgyu skar gyi dkyil 
[\[244a.4\]]'khor dang po'i sangs rgyas la lha'i tshogs rgyu skar gyi chu tshod kyi grangs kyi bdag nyid du bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs so/_/phyi rol du skar ma'i chu tshod rnams gza' thams cad kyi longs spyod yin pa ji lta ba de bzhin du nang du sku dang gsung dang thugs dang ye shes ni dbang po 
[\[244a.5\]]drug gi chos rnams kyi'o/_/gang gis rang gi lus la shes par gyur pa ste/_de ltar brjod pa'i rim pas shes rab dang thabs kyi bdag nyid dang po'i sangs rgyas gang gis rang gi lus la nyin mtshan dus thun mtshams dang thun tshod la sogs pa'i dbye ba dang zla ba 'pho ba'i dbye 
[\[244a.6\]]ba bcu gnyis rnams kyis shes par gyur pa'i rnal 'byor pa de ni dpal ldan 'jam pa'i rdo rje srid pa'i 'jigs 'joms sangs rgyas skye ba 'di nyid dag la'o zhes pa ni bsdus pa'i rgyud dang rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi lha 'byung ba'i nges pa'o/_/da ni sum cu zhes pa la sogs pas mchog gi dang po'i 
[\[244a.7\]]sangs rgyas 'di las bsdus pa'i rgyud 'byung ba gsungs te/_'dir mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas nyi shu lhag pa'i brgya phrag bcu drug las sum cu'i cha yis thob pa ni lha lnga bcu rtsa bzhir 'gyur ro/_/slar yang gsum gyis bsgyur ba ste sku dang gsung dang thugs kyis bsgyur ba 
[\[244b\]]
[\[244b.1\]]ni drug cu rtsa gnyis lhag pa'i brgyar 'gyur ro/_/de rnams las dkyil 'khor gyi lha ni lnga bcu rtsa bzhi lhag pa'i brgya'i grangs te dkyil 'khor gyi gtso bo thabs dang shes rab zung zhig dang phyi rol gyi phyag rgya drug go/_/bcu'i chas mchog gi dang po'i sangs rgyas las dus kyi 'khor

[\[244b.3\]]la rigs gsum gyi rtsa gsum ni lte ba dang gsang ba dang rdo rje nor bur lus dang ngag dang sems kyi thig le 'dzin pa rnams so/_/de bzhin du rigs drug gi rtsa rnams ni la la nA dang ra sa nA dang a ba d+hU tI dang bshang ba dang gci ba dang khu ba 'bab pa rnams so/_/de bzhin du rigs sum 
[\[244b.4\]]cu rtsa drug gi rtsa rnams ni gtsug tor la sogs pa 'khor lo drug gi rtsa drug dang /_sgra 'dzin pa gnyis dang rig pa 'dzin pa gnyis dang gzugs 'dzin pa gnyis dang ro 'dzin pa gnyis dang /_dri 'dzin pa gnyis po 'di dag ni rtsa bcu'o/_/lte ba'i 'khor lo'i nang dang 
[\[244b.5\]]gzhan gyi zlum po rnams la 'pho ba bcu gnyis kyi rtsa rnams dang thun tshod brgyad kyi rtsa rnams te/_de ltar thams cad rigs sum cu rtsa drug gi gtso mor 'gyur te ro'i rang bzhin can drug dang khams kyi rang bzhin can drug dang /_dbang po'i rang bzhin can drug dang /_yul gyi

[\[245a\]]
[\[245a.1\]]gtsug tor la ni chu gter zhes pa ni 'dab ma bzhi'i gzugs can bzhi ste thun mtshams bzhi 'jug par byed pa rnams so/_/snying khar brgyad de zhes pa ni rtsa snar ma la sogs pa mnyam gnas pa la sogs pa rnams kyi rten du gyur pa rnams te nyin zhag so sor thun gyi dbye bas

[\[245a.4\]]pa'o/_/nyis 'gyur mi bdag ces pa ni sum cu rtsa gnyis te gsang ba'i dbus su gsang ba'i pad+ma'i rtsa khu ba la sogs pa'i khams sum cu rtsa gnyis 'jug par byed pa rnams so/_/'di rnams las rtsa drug ni 'khor lo 'gog par 'gyur ro/_/bcus yul 'gog par 'gyur te de rnams 
[\[245a.5\]]ni 'dus pa'i rang bzhin no/_/slar yang de dag nyis 'gyur ni bad kan gyi cha ste nyi shu'o/_/slar yang nyis 'gyur ni mkhris pa'i cha ste bzhi bcur 'gyur ro/_/slar yang de dag rlung gi cha brgyad cur 'gyur te de ltar 'khor lo drug po rnams la rtsa lnga bcu rtsa drug lhag pa'i brgya rnam par

[\[245a.7\]]'byor pa rnams kyi 'di la ngo mtshar med do/_/gtsug tor la ni chu gter te bzhi'o/_/dpral bar rgya mtshos bsnun pa'i dus rnams zhes pa bcu drug ste rtsa nyi shu po 'di dag ni bad kan gyi khams rab tu 'khrug pa'o/_/mgrin par so dang zhes pa ni rtsa sum cu rtsa gnyis so/_/

[\[245b.1\]]snying kha'i chu skyes dag la mig gis bsgyur ba'i dus rnams zhes pa ni rtsa brgyad de rtsa bzhi bcu po 'di dag ni mkhris pa'i khams dgu rab tu 'khrug pa'o/_/lte ba dang gsang ba bdag la grangs bzhin du chu gter drug cu zhes pa ni rtsa drug cu rtsa bzhi'o/_/mi bdag dag kyang ste gsang

[\[273a.7\]]mtshan mo'i cha la chad pa'i ye shes lhag par gnas so/_/sangs rgyas pa dang lha min 'di dag gi grangs bzhin du sangs rgyas pa rnams kyi yang dag ye shes su 'gyur ro/_/de'i chos dang 'gal ba rnams nyams ni lha min rnams kyir 'gyur ro/_/yang dag ye shes ni nyin mo'i 
[\[273b\]]
[\[273b.1\]]snang ba bzhin du thams cad mthong ba'o/_/rnam nyams ye shes ni mtshan mo'i snang ba bzhin sems can rnams kyi 'chi ba dang 'tsho ba cung zad mthong ba'o/_/ji ltar shes she na/_de'i phyir gsungs pa/_chos bsten pa las so zhes pa ste/_'dir yang dag ye shes 
[\[273b.2\]]rnam nyams dag la tshig ni legs sbyar tha mal dag tu rab tu 'gyur ba ste zhes pa ni yang dag ye shes skyes pa rnams kyis legs par sbyar ba'i ngag sgra thams cad kyi bdag nyid do/_/rnam nyams ye shes skyes pa rnams kyi tha mal gyi ngag tu 'gyur te/_ston par byed pa 
[\[273b.3\]]po rnams kyi yul gcig gi skad gcig gi skad kyi khyad par gyis so/_/sangs rgyas pa rnams kyi ye shes ni sems can thams cad la snying rje'i bdag nyid can zhi ba'i las ston par byed pa'o/_/lha min rnams kyi ye shes ni sha bza' ba'i slad du dud 'gro'i 
[\[273b.4\]]sems can la gnod pa byed pa drag po'i las ston par byed pa po'o/_/srid pa gsum gyi gnas su sangs rgyas pa rnams kyi ye shes ni skyes bu nyid kyi las ston par byed do/_/lha min rnams kyi ye shes ni sa la tha mal gyi las ston par byed do/_/'byung po rnams 
[\[273b.5\]]kyi ni sa la rnam par 'dres pa'i las ston par byed do/_/ye shes ston par byed pa'i nges pa'o/_/da ni nyi ma gung zhes pa la sogs pas sangs rgyas pa dang lha min dag gi za ba'i dus gsungs te/_'dir nyin zhag so so la nyi ma gung nas brtsams nas mtshan mo phyed 
[\[273b.6\]]kyi bar du nyin mo'i dus so/_/mtshan mo phyed nas brtsams nas nyi ma gung gi mthar thug pa ni mtshan mo'i dus so/_/nyin mo dang mtshan mo'i dus de las rang rang gi dus kyi phyed phyi ma ni grangs bzhin du sangs rgyas pa dang /_kla klo de dag gi dka' thub pa rnams kyi za 
[\[273b.7\]]ba'i dus te khyim pa rnams kyi ni ma yin no zhes pa ste nges pa'o/_/sangs rgyas pa dang lha min dag gi bza' ba dang btung ba yang grangs bzhin du sangs rgyas pa rnams kyi zan khyad par du 'phags pa ste/_kla klo rnams kyi ba lang gi sha dang bcas pa'o/_/btung ba ni grangs 
[\[274a\]]
[\[274a.1\]]bzhin du sangs rgyas pa rnams kyi rnam mang bcud ster zhim pa ste/_kla klo rnams kyi btung ba ni bya gag la sogs pa'i sgo nga'i khu ba'o/_/bgo ba ni grangs bzhin du sangs rgyas pa rnams kyi gos dmar po ste/_kla klo rnams kyi ni dkar po'o/_/dka' thub pa rnams kyi ste khyim pa rnams 
[\[274a.2\]]kyi ni nges pa med do/_/de bzhin du shi ba'i mthar gnas ni grangs bzhin du nyi ma zla ba'i bgrod pa bzhin du zhes pa ste/_nyi ma steng du bgrod cing zla ba 'og tu bgrod do/_/de dag gi bgrod pa bzhin du mtho ris kyi gnas nyi ma'i bgrod pa ni sangs rgyas pa rnams kyi ste/_zla ba'i bgrod pa bzhin 
[\[274a.3\]]sa 'og gi gnas ni lha min rnams kyi'o/_/de bzhin du rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi dbang mdor bstan pa las bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs pa/_lus can thams cad shi ba na/_/zla ba bdud rtsi 'og tu 'gro/_/steng du nyi ma rdul sgra gcan/_/rnam shes dngos po'i mtshan nyid do/_/de bzhin du chos 
[\[274a.4\]]ni grangs bzhin du sangs rgyas pa rnams kyi 'tshe ba med pa'o/_/kla klo rnams kyi ni 'tshe ba ste yang yig las 'byung po rnams kyi'o/_/bla ma'i nges pa'i dbang gis bsgom pa'i dus dang 'dod pa'i lha la bstod pa'i dus su grangs bzhin du sangs rgyas pa rnams la rdo rje'i gdan bsngags shing kla klo 
[\[274a.5\]]rnams la lha min gyi gdan bsngags so/_/yang yig las gzhan ni thun mong ba ste de ni mi sgrub cing mi 'gog go/_/'dir lha min gyi gdan ni sa'i ngos la pus mo g.yon gyi steng du pus mo g.yas brkyang zhing rkang pa g.yon gyi steng du rkang pa g.yas pa'o/_/yang yig las 'og tu ste rkang pa g.yas pa'i 
[\[274a.6\]]steng du'o/_/rkang pa'i mthil du yang yang yig las rgyab tu dpyi bzhag pa'o/_/rdo rje'i gdan la sogs pa ni 'chad par 'gyur ba las brjod par bya'o/_/sangs rgyas pa dang lha min gyi bya ba nges pa'o/_/'byung po rnams kyi las 'dren mar sngar gsungs pa de yang 'dir tshigs su bcad pas gsal 
[\[274a.7\]]bar gsungs pa 'di lta ste/_'byung po rnams kyi las ni 'dren mar rab tu 'gyur te tshig kyang 'dres pa sha za dag gi'o/_/chos ni 'tshe med 'tshe ldan bla ma'i nges pa'i dbang gi srog gcod la sogs skyon dag la/_/mchod sbyin la sogs don du rab tu 'jug sogs pa'i sgras g.yul gyi don du 'tshe ba la rab 
[\[274b\]]
[\[274b.1\]]tu 'jug go/_/dka' thub kyi 'bras bu'i don du ldog pa ste/_mi 'tshe ba la rab tu 'jug par 'gyur te mi yi bdag po res 'ga' dag ni ldog pa'am zhes pa'o/_/bza' ba btung ba dag kyang 'dres pa nyi zla'i bgrod pa dag las lam ni 'dren ma gzhan du'o zhes pa ni/_'byung po rnams kyi lam 
[\[274b.2\]]ni thams cad kun du 'dres par rig par bya'o/_/'byung po rnams kyi bya ba'i nges pa'o/_/da ni 'od zer zhes pa la sogs pas lam brjod par 'dod pa gsungs te/_'dir lam ni rnam pa gsum ste 'od zer gyi lam dang du ba'i lam dang rnam par 'dres pa'i lam mo/_/'di dag las 'od zer lam ni nyi 
[\[274b.3\]]ma'i snang ba dkar po nyin mo bgrod pa byang ste nyin mo 'phel ba'i mtshan nyid dang lo phyed ni phyi rol du ste/_nang du chu srin la sogs pa dus sbyor drug gi bdag nyid can te/_shar ba nas nub pa zhes bya ba'i ming can no/_/du ba'i lam ni zla ba'i snang ba mtshan mo dag dang dkar min bgrod pa 'di ni lho ste/_du 
[\[274b.4\]]ba'i lam dag la karka Ta la sogs pa lhor bgrod pa'i zla ba drug ste/_gang du mtshan mo 'phel ba de lhor bgrod pa'o/_/nub par gyur pa nas shar ba'i bar ni nges pa'o/_/'dir 'od zer lha rnams mtho ris su ste mtho ris su 'gro ba'i lam mo/_/dkar min bgrod pa lha min rnams kyi klu'i 'jig rten du 
[\[274b.5\]]'gro ba'i du ba'i lam mo/_/kun du 'dres pa nyin mo dang mtshan mo'i chos can ni mi yi 'jig rten du 'gro ba la ste/[ {]yi dgas [,]yi dwags [}]dang dud 'gro dang mi rnams bskyed pa la rab tu gsal bar byas so zhes pa ni lam gyi nges pa ste/_skye ba snga ma'i bag chags kyis bskyed pa'i snying stobs dang mun pa dang rdul 
[\[274b.6\]]gyi yon tan gyi rang bzhin gyis so/_/da ni gang zhig gang gi dbus su zhes pa la sogs pas sangs rgyas pa'i nyan thos dang lha min dang 'byung po'i las la dga' ba'i sems can rnams kyi bag chags 'dzin pa gsungs te/_'khor ba 'dir skye ba'i las kyi bag chags kyi stobs kyis lha dang lha 
[\[274b.7\]]min dang 'byung po'i bya ba la rjes su chags pa gang zhig gang gi dbus su rab tu zhugs par gyur te/_brtul zhugs nges pa la dga' las kyi zhags pas des bcings pa/_/zhes pa chos de'i bya ba rnams kyis bcings pa'o/_/de yi dbus su rang rang bsam pas mi bdag ni bod pa ste/_de'i rigs 
[\[275a\]]
[\[275a.1\]]te lha dang lha min dang 'byung po'i rigs su de'i 'dzin pa dag gis zhes pa ni de'i dngos po'i 'dzin pa ste/_de yis lha dang lha min dang 'byung po'i rigs su skye bar 'gyur ro/_/snying stobs dang mun pa dang rdul gyi ngar 'dzin gyis te sngar brjod pa'i cho gas so/_/ji srid sems kyi 
[\[275a.2\]]rang bzhin dag ni rnam gsum srid pa'i dbang gis zhes pa ni 'dod pa dang gzugs dang gzugs med pa'i srid pa'i bag chags kyi dbang gis te/_tshor ba bde ba dang sdug bsngal 'khor ba par gyur pa de srid du 'khor ba sdug bsngal gyi mtshan nyid du sems kyang 'khyam ste/_mi yi bdag po mtho 
[\[275a.3\]]ris mi yul dag dang 'og tu yang zhes pa lha dang mi dang dmyal ba la sogs pa 'gro ba drug po rnams su kun gzhi'i rnam par shes pa 'khyam mo zhes pa ste bag chags 'dzin pa'i nges pa'o/_/de'i phyir bag chags rnam pa gsum las 'das pa ni 'khor ba'i pha rol mthar son pa/_bya ba byas 
[\[275a.4\]]pa skam sar gnas pa/_ye shes kyi sku bcom ldan 'das yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas lha dang lha ma yin dang klu la sogs pa rnams kyis zhabs kyi pad+ma la phyag byas pa dus kyi 'khor lo'o zhes pa ni bag chags spangs pa'i nges pa'o/_/da ni ka sogs zhes pa la sogs pas mngal du 
[\[275a.5\]]gsal byed dang dbyangs kyang skye bar 'gyur ba ji lta ba de bzhin du 'chi ba'i mtshan nyid gsungs te/_'dir mngal 'dzin pa'i dus su rdul ni gsal byed sum cu'i bdag nyid dang khu ba ni dbyangs sum cu'i bdag nyid de 'di dag kun gzhi'i rnam par shes pas byin gyis brlabs pa ni yang dag par sbyor 
[\[275a.6\]]ro/_/'dir mngal du skye ba'i phyogs dang po la ka yig la sogs pa'i gsal byed sum cu po rnams dbyangs thung ngus byin gyis brlabs pa dang /_phyogs phyi ma la dbyangs ring pos byin gyis brlabs pa ste gsal byed kyi phyogs gnyis po 'di ni 'chi ba la rab tu gsal bar 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin 
[\[275a.7\]]du dkar po'i phyogs la khu ba'i nam mkha'i khams rdul la rab tu 'jug ste/_nag po'i phyogs la sa'i khams rab tu 'jug go/_/de bzhin du 'byung ba dang mi 'byung ba dag la rdul gyi sa dang nam mkha'i khams dag khu ba'i khams la rab tu 'jug go/_/de bzhin du zla ba gnyis pa'i phyogs 
[\[275b\]]
[\[275b.1\]]dang po la i yig gis byin gyis brlabs pa'i gsal byed rnams te rdul la rlung gi khams rab tu 'jug go/_/phyogs phyi ma la u yig gis byin gyis brlabs pa rnams te chu'i khams 'jug go/_/gsum pa'i phyogs dang po la me'i khams 'jug pa'i slad du r-i yig gis byin gyis 
[\[275b.2\]]brlabs pa rnams so/_/phyogs phyi ma la me'i khams 'jug pa'i slad du r-I yig gis byin gyis brlabs pa rnams so/_/zla ba bzhi pa'i phyogs dang po la chu'i khams 'jug pa'i slad du u yig gis byin gyis brlabs pa rnams so/_/phyogs phyi ma la rlung gi khams 'jug pa'i slad du I yig 
[\[275b.3\]]gis byin gyis brlabs pa rnams so/_/zla ba lnga pa'i phyogs dang po la sa'i khams 'jug pa'i slad du l-i yig gis byin gyis brlabs pa rnams so/_/phyogs phyi ma la nam mkha'i khams 'jug pa'i slad du A yig ring pos byin gyis brlabs pa rnams te/_skyed pa dang sdud pa'i dbye bas nam 
[\[275b.4\]]mkha' la sogs pa dang sa la sogs pa khu ba dang rdul dag phan tshun du 'jug go/_/de nas dbang po dang las kyi dbang po byin gyis brlabs pa'i slad du yon tan dang 'phel ba dang ha la sogs pa thung ngu dang ring po rnams gsal byed rnams la 'jug par byed de/_zla ba drug pa'i phyogs dang po'i 
[\[275b.5\]]phyed la a/_phyogs dang po'i phyed phyi ma la ha/_phyogs phyi ma'i phyed la Al/_phyogs phyi ma'i phyed phyi ma la lA/_de bzhin du bdun pa la e ya au wA/_/brgyad pa la ar ra Ar rA/_dgu pa la o wa ai yA/_bcu pa la al la A hA zhes pa gsal byed sum cu po rnams la 'jug go/_/
[\[275b.6\]]mngal gyi zla ba'i rjes la zla ba bcus phung po dang khams dang skye mched la sogs pa rnams 'grub bo/_/khu ba dang rdul yang dag par sbyor bas phung po dang khams dang skye mched la sogs pa rnams spro ste skyed pa dang sdud pa'i rim pas so/_/de bzhin du zla ba bcu gcig pa la thig les byin gyis brlabs 
[\[275b.7\]]pa sum cu dang /_zla ba bcu gnyis pa la rnam par bcad pas byin gyis brlabs pa rnams su 'gyur ro/_/de nas zla ba'i khams khu ba'i khams kyi rtsa dang rkang dang rus pa rnams skyed pa yongs su chad de shes rab kyi lo bcu gnyis dang thabs kyi lo bcu drug la nyi ma rdul 'phel bar byed do/_/de nas ye shes 
[\[276a\]]
[\[276a.1\]]kyi khams smin te/_shes rab kyi rdul smin zhing thabs kyi khu ba smin no/_/de nas snga ma bzhin du shes rab kyi gzhan lo bcu gnyis dang thabs kyi gzhan lo bcu drug tu sa'i khams smin no/_/mtshams 'di nas snying stobs kyi cha dbu mar 'gyur ro/_/slar yang shes rab kyi gzhan lo 
[\[276a.2\]]bco brgyad du chu'i khams smin te/_thabs kyi phyed dang bcas pa'i nyin zhag nyi shu rtsa gnyis dang /_zla ba gcig lhag pa'i lo bcu drug tu snga ma bzhin du chu'i khams smin no/_/shes rab kyi me smin pa ni snga ma bzhin te thabs kyi yang snga ma bzhin no/_/de bzhin du rlung gi khams smin pa dang nam 
[\[276a.3\]]mkha'i khams smin pa ste snying stobs dang rdul dang mun pa'i dbye bas lo dgu bcu rtsa drug dang phyed dang bcas pa'i zla ba bcus khams thams cad smin no/_/de nas dbu ma'i rtsa lo dang phyogs dang nyin zhag gi tshad gsal byed sum cu'i tshad de nyin zhag gi tshogs lnga bcu rtsa lnga lhag pa'i brgya phrag 
[\[276a.4\]]bcu gcig gis dus rab tu rgyu ste za bar byed do/_/ka sogs sde rnams dbyangs dang bcas pa sngar brjod pa dag kyang yon tan gyis bsgyur ba nyin zhag stong brgyad cur gyur pa dang gang yang phyogs gsum dag gi dbyangs thung ngu dang ring po dang shin tu ring po bzhi bcu rtsa lnga dang /_nges par sum cu'i 
[\[276a.5\]]gsal byed de dag rnams la rnam gsum bgrod pa'i dbang gis zhes pa g.yon dang g.yas dang dbu ma'i bgrod pa'i dbang gis 'chi ba rgyu'o/_/de bzhin du bkra shis la sogs pa gza' lnga'i rkang pa sum cu rtsa bzhi lhag pa'i brgya phrag bcu gcig sngar brjod pa rnams dang nyi ma ri bong can gyi rkang pa ro 
[\[276a.6\]]dang dus dang ri bong can te rkang pa bcu gcig gi bdag nyid dang /_sgra gcan mjug [{]rings [,]ring [}]rkang pa ste sgra gcan gyi rkang pa ni phyed dang bcas pa'i bdun gyi bdag nyid de zla ba yongs su bcad pa'i bdag nyid do/_/mjug [{]rings [,]ring [}]'char ba'i rkang pa ni nyin zhag so sor chu tshod gnyis kyi bdag nyid de rkang pa 
[\[276a.7\]]chu tshod kyi bdag nyid 'di dag ni lo gsum dang phyogs gsum dang gsal byed sum cu'i nyin zhag gi grangs te 'di dag nyid la 'chi ba rgyu'o/_/de bzhin du sum cu rtsa gnyis lo dang phyogs kyang yon tan gyis bsgyur ba zla ba nyi ma me yis phye ba'o zhes pa ni snying stobs dang rdul dang mun pas phye ba ste lo dgu 
[\[276b\]]
[\[276b.1\]]bcu rtsa drug dang gzhan phyed dang bcas pa'i zla ba bcu'i phyogs rnams so/_/g.yon dang g.yas kyi rtsa'i phyogs lo brgya'i phyogs rnams kyi nang nas lo gsum gyi ni phyogs rnams dag ste bdun cu rtsa gnyis dang /_ri bong can gyi yon tan de phyogs gsum dang dus kyi me yi phyogs gnyis dag ste/_
[\[276b.2\]]de ltar phyogs bdun cu rtsa bdun lo brgya las ni ste lo brgya'i phyogs kyi phung po brgya phrag nyi shu rtsa bzhi'i grangs las dman par gyur pa'o/_/lhag ma'i phyogs ni nyi zla dag gi ste g.yas dang g.yon gyi'o/_/'jig rten mig dang me mig grangs zhes pa nyi shu rtsa gsum lhag pa'i brgya phrag nyi shu rtsa 
[\[276b.3\]]gsum mo/_/de bzhin du nyin mtshan dus rnams gtso bo dag gis dman pa mtha' dag ste lo brgya'i stong phrag sum cu rtsa drug go/_/gtso bos dman pa ni lo gsum dang phyogs gsum dang gsal byed kyi zla bas dman pa ste g.yon dang g.yas la 'chi bas zos pa'o/_/gsal byed la sogs sde 
[\[276b.4\]]pa dbyangs rnams dag ni nyi ma zla ba'i bgrod pa'i dbang gis 'chi bas zos te/_'chi ltas nas brtsams nas dbu ma'i dus su zos so/_/de bzhin du phyed bcas 'khor lo'i chu tshod phyed bcas 'khor lo ni khyim bco brgyad kyi grangs te sum cu rtsa lnga lhag pa'i brgyas bsgyur ba sum cu lhag pa'i brgya 
[\[276b.5\]]phrag nyi shu rtsa bzhi ni chu tshod kyi grangs so/_/ri bong can gyi rkang pa ni rim pa dang rim pa ma yin pa'i dbye bas 'byung dang 'byung ba mda' dang rig byed la sogs pa brgya'i grangs te/_de dag dang bral ba ni ri bong can gyi rkang pa dang bral ba'o/_/gza'i chu tshod ni bdun te des dman pa mtha' dag ni nyi shu rtsa gsum lhag 
[\[276b.6\]]pa'i brgya phrag nyi shu rtsa gsum gyi grangs so/_/shes rab thabs kyi rab tu dbye ba dag gis te sdud pa dang skyed pa'i dbye bas nyin mtshan dus kyis de dag rnams kyang 'chi bas zos so/_/de ltar sde pa dbyangs bcas dbyangs kyi tshogs dang bcas pa gsal byed rnams nyid dag dang rkang pa ni/_dman dang bar ma dus 'chi zla ba 
[\[276b.7\]]nyi ma'i 'chi ba dang ni gsal byed lhag pa'i lha la'o zhes pa ni dman pa zla ba'i 'chi ltas kyi 'chi ba dang /_bar ma nyi ma'i 'chi ltas kyi 'chi ba dang /_gsal byed lhag pa'i lha dbu ma'i dus su 'chi ba'i 'chi ba la ste sngar brjod pa'i nges pa'o/_/de nas sum cu gnyis dang nyin zhag gcig kyang zhes pa nyin zhag sum cu rtsa 
[\[277a\]]
[\[277a.1\]]gsum mo/_/ri bong can bcas nyi ma'i bgrod la ste g.yon pa'i bgrod pa dang bcas pa'i g.yas pa'i bgrod pa las rtsa ni gcod pa 'di thun mong ba'o/_/dus kyi 'chi ba la ni slar yang ste sum cu mtshan mo gsum gyis zhes pa nyin zhag sum cu rtsa gsum gyis rdo rje dang bcas te lus dang ngag dang sems dang bcas 
[\[277a.2\]]pa'i phung po khams dang dbang po la sogs 'dor ro/_/de nas dor nas zla ba nyi ma'i rtsa la la nA dang ra sa nA dag dang dung can ma a ba d+hU tIr srog gi rlung dag nyin gcig rab 'jug ste sa dang chu dang bsreg za rlung dang nam mkha'i khams ni rnam par chad pa ji srid nyid du'o/_/'dir 'chi ba'i nyin zhag la dbu mar srog rab tu 
[\[277a.3\]]zhugs te dus kyi rlung gis dman par gyur pa na lte bar sa'i khams yon tan lnga'i rang bzhin 'dor te de nas sa'i khams rnam par chad par 'gyur ro/_/snying gar chu'i khams yon tan bzhi'i rang bzhin 'dor te chu'i khams rnam par chad par 'gyur ro/_/mgrin par me'i khams yon tan gsum gyi rang bzhin 'dor te de nas 
[\[277a.4\]]me'i khams rnam par chad par 'gyur ro/_/dpral bar rlung gi khams yon tan gnyis kyi rang bzhin 'dor te de nas rlung gi khams rnam par chad par 'gyur ro/_/gtsug tor du nam mkha'i khams yon tan gcig gi rang bzhin 'dor te de nas stong pa'i khams rnam par 'chad do zhes pa ni steng du srog gi khams yongs su 'dor 
[\[277a.5\]]ba'o/_/'og tu lte bar lus kyi thig le'i chos thur sel 'dor ro/_/de nas sad pa'i gnas skabs zad par 'gyur ro/_/gsang bar ngag gi thig le'i chos 'dor te/_de nas rmi lam gyi gnas skabs chad par 'gyur ro/_/nor bur sems kyi thig le'i chos 'dor te de nas shin tu gnyid log pa'i gnas skabs chad par 'gyur ro/_/de bzhin du steng 
[\[277a.6\]]du yang rdul gyi khams kyi thig le rnams srog gis 'dor te/_de nas lus dang ngag dang sems ni rnam par chad par 'gyur ro/_/'pho ba'i dus su drug pa ye shes kyi khams 'dor ro/_/yan lag thams cad la khu ba 'og tu 'dor zhing rdul steng du 'dor te bzhi pa'i gnas skabs rnam par chad par 'gyur ro/_/de ltar mngal nas skyes pa 
[\[277a.7\]]rnams 'chi ba'i dus nges pa'o/_/bsdus pa'i rgyud kyi rgyal po dus kyi 'khor lo'i 'grel bshad rtsa ba'i rgyud kyi rjes su 'jug pa stong phrag bcu gnyis pa dri ma med pa'i 'od las 'jig rten kun rdzob kyi skye ba dang 'gag pa'i rgyur gyur pa skad cig gi mtshan nyid dus kyi 'khor lo nges pa'i mdor bsdus chen po ste lnga pa'o//_//
